    <div class="PagePromo-content">
        
    <div class="PagePromo-title">
        <a class="Link" href="https://www.nbcsports.com/nfl/profootballtalk/rumor-mill/news/report-jaguars-ot-anton-harrison-to-have-knee-surgery">Report: Jaguars OT Anton Harrison to have knee surgery</a>
    </div>



        
    <div class="PagePromo-description"><span>The Jaguars won’t have left tackle Anton Harrison in the lineup against the Texans in Week 6 and it’s unclear when he will be back in action. </span></div>
